**本章主线：** 国家制度 → 公共机构 → 权力与组织 → 人口迁移 → 选举政策 → 社会福祉

**最后尝试回答：** 选择一项公共议题：哪些机构参与，谁受影响，怎样提议、实施与监督？

### 词群 15.01｜国家怎样组织与改变 {#group-15-01}

**串联方式：** 先辨政治思想与政体，再讨论政府权威、等级、民主、官僚体制及改革。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-001">
<summary><h4 id="word-15-001" class="vocabulary-word-title">republic<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-15-001" aria-label="定位 republic">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="15-001" data-mark-label="republic" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="republic：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="republic：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=republic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 republic" title="在有道查询 republic">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">15-001</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.共和国，共和政体</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">In the fictional <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=republic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>republic</strong></a>, the new government challenged the authority of the old political regime.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">在这个虚构共和国，新政府挑战了旧政治体制的权力。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=republic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>republic</strong></a>’s constitution defines the government’s authority and the limits of the political regime.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">共和国宪法规定政府权力及政治体制的边界。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A change of political regime in the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=republic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>republic</strong></a> transferred authority to an elected government.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">共和国政治体制的改变把权力转移给民选政府。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a democratic republic</span> · 民主共和国</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=constitution&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">constitution</a></span> · 宪法</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=governance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">governance</a></span> · 治理</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-002">
<summary><h4 id="word-15-002" class="vocabulary-word-title">Marxism<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-15-002" aria-label="定位 Marxism">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="15-002" data-mark-label="Marxism" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="Marxism：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="Marxism：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=Marxism&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 Marxism" title="在有道查询 Marxism">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">15-002</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.马克思主义</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The course compares <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=Marxism&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Marxism</strong></a>, socialism and communism and introduces historical materialism.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这门课比较马克思主义、社会主义与共产主义，并介绍历史唯物主义。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Her essay examines the role of materialism in <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=Marxism&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Marxism</strong></a> and distinguishes socialism from communism.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她的论文考察唯物主义在马克思主义中的作用，并区分社会主义与共产主义。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Students discussed how <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=Marxism&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Marxism</strong></a> uses historical materialism when analysing the development of socialism and communism.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">学生讨论了马克思主义在分析社会主义与共产主义发展时如何运用历史唯物主义。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">the study of Marxism</span> · 马克思主义研究</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=political%20theory&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">political theory</a></span> · 政治理论</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=class%20analysis&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">class analysis</a></span> · 阶级分析</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-003">
<summary><h4 id="word-15-003" class="vocabulary-word-title">socialism<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-15-003" aria-label="定位 socialism">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="15-003" data-mark-label="socialism" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="socialism：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="socialism：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=socialism&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 socialism" title="在有道查询 socialism">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">15-003</span></span></summary>

<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.社会主义</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The course compares Marxism, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=socialism&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>socialism</strong></a> and communism and introduces historical materialism.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这门课比较马克思主义、社会主义与共产主义，并介绍历史唯物主义。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Her essay examines the role of materialism in Marxism and distinguishes <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=socialism&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>socialism</strong></a> from communism.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她的论文考察唯物主义在马克思主义中的作用，并区分社会主义与共产主义。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Students discussed how Marxism uses historical materialism when analysing the development of <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=socialism&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>socialism</strong></a> and communism.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">学生讨论了马克思主义在分析社会主义与共产主义发展时如何运用历史唯物主义。</p></li>
</ol>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">In the fictional republic, the new government challenged the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=authority&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>authority</strong></a> of the old political regime.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">在这个虚构共和国，新政府挑战了旧政治体制的权力。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The republic’s constitution defines the government’s <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=authority&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>authority</strong></a> and the limits of the political regime.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">共和国宪法规定政府权力及政治体制的边界。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A change of political regime in the republic transferred <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=authority&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>authority</strong></a> to an elected government.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">共和国政治体制的改变把权力转移给民选政府。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">legal authority</span> · 法律权力</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=constitution&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">constitution</a></span> · 宪法</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=governance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">governance</a></span> · 治理</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-008">
<summary><h4 id="word-15-008" class="vocabulary-word-title">political<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-15-008" aria-label="定位 political">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="15-008" data-mark-label="political" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="political：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="political：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=political&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 political" title="在有道查询 political">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">15-008</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj.政治的；政党的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">In the fictional republic, the new government challenged the authority of the old <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=political&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>political</strong></a> regime.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">在这个虚构共和国，新政府挑战了旧政治体制的权力。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The republic’s constitution defines the government’s authority and the limits of the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=political&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>political</strong></a> regime.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">共和国宪法规定政府权力及政治体制的边界。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A change of <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=political&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>political</strong></a> regime in the republic transferred authority to an elected government.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">共和国政治体制的改变把权力转移给民选政府。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">political reform</span> · 政治改革</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=constitution&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">constitution</a></span> · 宪法</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=governance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">governance</a></span> · 治理</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-009">
<summary><h4 id="word-15-009" class="vocabulary-word-title">hierarchy<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-15-009" aria-label="定位 hierarchy">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="15-009" data-mark-label="hierarchy" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="hierarchy：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="hierarchy：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hierarchy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 hierarchy" title="在有道查询 hierarchy">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">15-009</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.等级制度；统治集团</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An egalitarian democracy may still need an administrative <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hierarchy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hierarchy</strong></a>, but excessive bureaucracy can weaken participation.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">平等主义民主国家可能仍需要行政等级体系，但过多官僚程序会削弱参与。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The reform aimed to make the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hierarchy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hierarchy</strong></a> less rigid, reduce bureaucracy and give democracy a more egalitarian character.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">改革旨在弱化僵硬等级、减少官僚程序，并使民主更具平等主义特征。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Students debated whether an egalitarian democracy could function without any <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hierarchy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hierarchy</strong></a> or bureaucracy.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">学生辩论平等主义民主制度能否在完全没有等级或官僚机构的情况下运作。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">an administrative hierarchy</span> · 行政等级体系</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=participation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">participation</a></span> · 参与</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=accountability&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">accountability</a></span> · 问责</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-010">
<summary><h4 id="word-15-010" class="vocabulary-word-title">democracy<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-15-010" aria-label="定位 democracy">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="15-010" data-mark-label="democracy" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="democracy：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="democracy：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=democracy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 democracy" title="在有道查询 democracy">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">15-010</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.民主，民主制度；民主国家</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An egalitarian <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=democracy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>democracy</strong></a> may still need an administrative hierarchy, but excessive bureaucracy can weaken participation.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">平等主义民主国家可能仍需要行政等级体系，但过多官僚程序会削弱参与。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The reform aimed to make the hierarchy less rigid, reduce bureaucracy and give <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=democracy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>democracy</strong></a> a more egalitarian character.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">改革旨在弱化僵硬等级、减少官僚程序，并使民主更具平等主义特征。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Students debated whether an egalitarian <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=democracy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>democracy</strong></a> could function without any hierarchy or bureaucracy.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">学生辩论平等主义民主制度能否在完全没有等级或官僚机构的情况下运作。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">parliamentary democracy</span> · 议会民主制</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=participation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">participation</a></span> · 参与</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=accountability&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">accountability</a></span> · 问责</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-011">
<summary><h4 id="word-15-011" class="vocabulary-word-title">bureaucracy<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-15-011" aria-label="定位 bureaucracy">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="15-011" data-mark-label="bureaucracy" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="bureaucracy：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="bureaucracy：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bureaucracy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 bureaucracy" title="在有道查询 bureaucracy">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">15-011</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.官僚制度；官僚作风</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An egalitarian democracy may still need an administrative hierarchy, but excessive <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bureaucracy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bureaucracy</strong></a> can weaken participation.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">平等主义民主国家可能仍需要行政等级体系，但过多官僚程序会削弱参与。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The reform aimed to make the hierarchy less rigid, reduce <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bureaucracy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bureaucracy</strong></a> and give democracy a more egalitarian character.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">改革旨在弱化僵硬等级、减少官僚程序，并使民主更具平等主义特征。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Students debated whether an egalitarian democracy could function without any hierarchy or <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bureaucracy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bureaucracy</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">学生辩论平等主义民主制度能否在完全没有等级或官僚机构的情况下运作。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">reduce bureaucracy</span> · 减少官僚程序</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=participation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">participation</a></span> · 参与</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=accountability&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">accountability</a></span> · 问责</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-012">
<summary><h4 id="word-15-012" class="vocabulary-word-title">egalitarian<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-15-012" aria-label="定位 egalitarian">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="15-012" data-mark-label="egalitarian" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="egalitarian：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="egalitarian：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=egalitarian&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 egalitarian" title="在有道查询 egalitarian">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">15-012</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj.平等主义的，；n.平等主义者</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=egalitarian&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>egalitarian</strong></a> democracy may still need an administrative hierarchy, but excessive bureaucracy can weaken participation.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">平等主义民主国家可能仍需要行政等级体系，但过多官僚程序会削弱参与。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The reform aimed to make the hierarchy less rigid, reduce bureaucracy and give democracy a more <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=egalitarian&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>egalitarian</strong></a> character.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">改革旨在弱化僵硬等级、减少官僚程序，并使民主更具平等主义特征。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Students debated whether an <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=egalitarian&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>egalitarian</strong></a> democracy could function without any hierarchy or bureaucracy.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">学生辩论平等主义民主制度能否在完全没有等级或官僚机构的情况下运作。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">egalitarian values</span> · 平等主义价值观</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=participation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">participation</a></span> · 参与</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=accountability&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">accountability</a></span> · 问责</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-013">
<summary><h4 id="word-15-013" class="vocabulary-word-title">materialism<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-15-013" aria-label="定位 materialism">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="15-013" data-mark-label="materialism" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="materialism：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="materialism：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=materialism&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 materialism" title="在有道查询 materialism">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">15-013</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.唯物主义；唯物论</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The course compares Marxism, socialism and communism and introduces historical <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=materialism&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>materialism</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这门课比较马克思主义、社会主义与共产主义，并介绍历史唯物主义。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Her essay examines the role of <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=materialism&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>materialism</strong></a> in Marxism and distinguishes socialism from communism.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她的论文考察唯物主义在马克思主义中的作用，并区分社会主义与共产主义。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Students discussed how Marxism uses historical <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=materialism&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>materialism</strong></a> when analysing the development of socialism and communism.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">学生讨论了马克思主义在分析社会主义与共产主义发展时如何运用历史唯物主义。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">historical materialism</span> · 历史唯物主义</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=political%20theory&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">political theory</a></span> · 政治理论</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=class%20analysis&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">class analysis</a></span> · 阶级分析</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-014">
<summary><h4 id="word-15-014" class="vocabulary-word-title">revolution<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-15-014" aria-label="定位 revolution">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="15-014" data-mark-label="revolution" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="revolution：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="revolution：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=revolution&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 revolution" title="在有道查询 revolution">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">15-014</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.革命；巨大变化</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">After the financial meltdown, conservative leaders chose a gradual reform process rather than <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=revolution&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>revolution</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">金融崩溃后，保守派领导人选择渐进改革过程，而非革命。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=revolution&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>revolution</strong></a> began after a meltdown weakened the state, while conservative groups demanded a slower reform process.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">国家因崩溃而衰弱后革命爆发，保守团体则要求更缓慢的改革进程。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A conservative minister argued that a careful reform process could prevent both economic meltdown and political <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=revolution&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>revolution</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一位保守派部长主张，谨慎的改革进程能防止经济崩溃和政治革命。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a political revolution</span> · 政治革命</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=transition&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">transition</a></span> · 转型</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=stability&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">stability</a></span> · 稳定</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-015">

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">After the financial meltdown, conservative leaders chose a gradual <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reform&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>reform</strong></a> process rather than revolution.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">金融崩溃后，保守派领导人选择渐进改革过程，而非革命。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The revolution began after a meltdown weakened the state, while conservative groups demanded a slower <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reform&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>reform</strong></a> process.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">国家因崩溃而衰弱后革命爆发，保守团体则要求更缓慢的改革进程。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A conservative minister argued that a careful <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reform&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>reform</strong></a> process could prevent both economic meltdown and political revolution.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一位保守派部长主张，谨慎的改革进程能防止经济崩溃和政治革命。</p></li>
</ol>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The revolution began after a meltdown weakened the state, while conservative groups demanded a slower reform <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=process&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>process</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">国家因崩溃而衰弱后革命爆发，保守团体则要求更缓慢的改革进程。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A conservative minister argued that a careful reform <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=process&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>process</strong></a> could prevent both economic meltdown and political revolution.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一位保守派部长主张，谨慎的改革进程能防止经济崩溃和政治革命。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-017">
<summary><h4 id="word-15-017" class="vocabulary-word-title">conservative<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-15-017" aria-label="定位 conservative">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="15-017" data-mark-label="conservative" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="conservative：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="conservative：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=conservative&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 conservative" title="在有道查询 conservative">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">15-017</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj.保守的；n.保守党党员</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">After the financial meltdown, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=conservative&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>conservative</strong></a> leaders chose a gradual reform process rather than revolution.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">金融崩溃后，保守派领导人选择渐进改革过程，而非革命。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The revolution began after a meltdown weakened the state, while <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=conservative&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>conservative</strong></a> groups demanded a slower reform process.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">国家因崩溃而衰弱后革命爆发，保守团体则要求更缓慢的改革进程。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=conservative&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>conservative</strong></a> minister argued that a careful reform process could prevent both economic meltdown and political revolution.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一位保守派部长主张，谨慎的改革进程能防止经济崩溃和政治革命。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a conservative approach</span> · 保守做法</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=transition&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">transition</a></span> · 转型</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=stability&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">stability</a></span> · 稳定</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-018">

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. (核反应堆的)堆芯熔毁；崩溃</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">After the financial <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=meltdown&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>meltdown</strong></a>, conservative leaders chose a gradual reform process rather than revolution.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">金融崩溃后，保守派领导人选择渐进改革过程，而非革命。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The revolution began after a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=meltdown&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>meltdown</strong></a> weakened the state, while conservative groups demanded a slower reform process.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">国家因崩溃而衰弱后革命爆发，保守团体则要求更缓慢的改革进程。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A conservative minister argued that a careful reform process could prevent both economic <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=meltdown&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>meltdown</strong></a> and political revolution.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一位保守派部长主张，谨慎的改革进程能防止经济崩溃和政治革命。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a financial meltdown</span> · 金融崩溃</p>

</div>

### 词群 15.02｜地方治理与国际交往背景 {#group-15-02}

**串联方式：** 从市政进入中立、双边关系和活动舞台，再辨国旗、旗帜等象征。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-019">
<summary><h4 id="word-15-019" class="vocabulary-word-title">municipal<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-15-019" aria-label="定位 municipal">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="15-019" data-mark-label="municipal" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="municipal：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="municipal：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=municipal&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 municipal" title="在有道查询 municipal">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">15-019</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj.市政的，地方政府的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=municipal&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>municipal</strong></a> delegation remained neutral in the dispute while hosting bilateral talks in an international arena.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">市政代表团在争端中保持中立，同时在国际舞台上主持双边会谈。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=municipal&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Municipal</strong></a> leaders sought a neutral venue for bilateral cooperation beyond the national political arena.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">市政领导人为超越国内政治领域的双边合作寻找中立场地。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A neutral mediator helped <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=municipal&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>municipal</strong></a> officials bring their bilateral agreement into the wider regional arena.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">中立调解人帮助市政官员把双边协议带入更广泛的区域合作领域。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">municipal government</span> · 市政府</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=diplomacy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">diplomacy</a></span> · 外交</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mediation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">mediation</a></span> · 调解</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Municipal leaders sought a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=neutral&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>neutral</strong></a> venue for bilateral cooperation beyond the national political arena.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">市政领导人为超越国内政治领域的双边合作寻找中立场地。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=neutral&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>neutral</strong></a> mediator helped municipal officials bring their bilateral agreement into the wider regional arena.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">中立调解人帮助市政官员把双边协议带入更广泛的区域合作领域。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-021">

<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj.双边的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The municipal delegation remained neutral in the dispute while hosting <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bilateral&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bilateral</strong></a> talks in an international arena.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">市政代表团在争端中保持中立，同时在国际舞台上主持双边会谈。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Municipal leaders sought a neutral venue for <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bilateral&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bilateral</strong></a> cooperation beyond the national political arena.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">市政领导人为超越国内政治领域的双边合作寻找中立场地。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A neutral mediator helped municipal officials bring their <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bilateral&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bilateral</strong></a> agreement into the wider regional arena.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">中立调解人帮助市政官员把双边协议带入更广泛的区域合作领域。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">bilateral talks</span> · 双边会谈</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=diplomacy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">diplomacy</a></span> · 外交</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mediation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">mediation</a></span> · 调解</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

### 词群 15.03｜政府由谁在何处运作 {#group-15-03}

**串联方式：** 把领导职位、议会与部门连到会议、总部和代表团，再讨论公共服务和行政管理。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-025">
<summary><h4 id="word-15-025" class="vocabulary-word-title">president<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-15-025" aria-label="定位 president">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="15-025" data-mark-label="president" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="president：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="president：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=president&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 president" title="在有道查询 president">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">15-025</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 总统；校长；董事长</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=president&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>president</strong></a> met the premier and finance minister while a secretary recorded the discussion.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">总统会见总理与财政部长，一名秘书记录讨论。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A secretary arranged separate meetings for the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=president&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>president</strong></a>, the premier and the transport minister.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">秘书分别为总统、总理和交通部长安排了会议。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The premier sent the minister to brief the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=president&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>president</strong></a>, accompanied by a senior secretary.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">总理派部长向总统汇报，一名高级秘书陪同。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a newly elected president</span> · 新当选总统</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=executive&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">executive</a></span> · 行政部门</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cabinet&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">cabinet</a></span> · 内阁</li></ul>

</details>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The president met the premier and finance minister while a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=secretary&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>secretary</strong></a> recorded the discussion.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">总统会见总理与财政部长，一名秘书记录讨论。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=secretary&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>secretary</strong></a> arranged separate meetings for the president, the premier and the transport minister.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">秘书分别为总统、总理和交通部长安排了会议。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The premier sent the minister to brief the president, accompanied by a senior <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=secretary&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>secretary</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">总理派部长向总统汇报，一名高级秘书陪同。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a government secretary</span> · 政府秘书</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=executive&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">executive</a></span> · 行政部门</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cabinet&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">cabinet</a></span> · 内阁</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-029">
<summary><h4 id="word-15-029" class="vocabulary-word-title">parliament<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-15-029" aria-label="定位 parliament">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="15-029" data-mark-label="parliament" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="parliament：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="parliament：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=parliament&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 parliament" title="在有道查询 parliament">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">15-029</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 议会，国会；［P～] (英国)议会</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The senate committee held a meeting before the conference on <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=parliament&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>parliament</strong></a>’s reform powers.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">参议院委员会在讨论议会改革权力的会议前先开了会。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Members of <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=parliament&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>parliament</strong></a> joined the senate delegation at a conference following their morning meeting.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">议员在上午会议后与参议院代表团一同参加了大会。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The conference ended with a joint meeting of the two chambers of <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=parliament&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>parliament</strong></a>, including the senate.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">大会以议会两院联席会议结束，其中包括参议院。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a parliamentary debate</span> · 议会辩论</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=legislature&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">legislature</a></span> · 立法机关</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=chamber&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">chamber</a></span> · 议院</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-030">
<summary><h4 id="word-15-030" class="vocabulary-word-title">senate<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-15-030" aria-label="定位 senate">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="15-030" data-mark-label="senate" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="senate：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="senate：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=senate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 senate" title="在有道查询 senate">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">15-030</span></span></summary>

<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 大学理事会；［the S～](美国等国家的)参议院</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=senate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>senate</strong></a> committee held a meeting before the conference on parliament’s reform powers.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">参议院委员会在讨论议会改革权力的会议前先开了会。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Members of parliament joined the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=senate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>senate</strong></a> delegation at a conference following their morning meeting.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">议员在上午会议后与参议院代表团一同参加了大会。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The conference ended with a joint meeting of the two chambers of parliament, including the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=senate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>senate</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">大会以议会两院联席会议结束，其中包括参议院。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a senate committee</span> · 参议院委员会</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=legislature&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">legislature</a></span> · 立法机关</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=chamber&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">chamber</a></span> · 议院</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-031">
<summary><h4 id="word-15-031" class="vocabulary-word-title">conference<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-15-031" aria-label="定位 conference">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="15-031" data-mark-label="conference" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="conference：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="conference：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=conference&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 conference" title="在有道查询 conference">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">15-031</span></span></summary>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The senate committee held a meeting before the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=conference&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>conference</strong></a> on parliament’s reform powers.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">参议院委员会在讨论议会改革权力的会议前先开了会。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Members of parliament joined the senate delegation at a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=conference&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>conference</strong></a> following their morning meeting.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">议员在上午会议后与参议院代表团一同参加了大会。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=conference&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>conference</strong></a> ended with a joint meeting of the two chambers of parliament, including the senate.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">大会以议会两院联席会议结束，其中包括参议院。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a policy conference</span> · 政策会议</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=legislature&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">legislature</a></span> · 立法机关</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=chamber&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">chamber</a></span> · 议院</li></ul>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-032">

<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.会议；会面</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The senate committee held a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=meeting&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>meeting</strong></a> before the conference on parliament’s reform powers.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">参议院委员会在讨论议会改革权力的会议前先开了会。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Members of parliament joined the senate delegation at a conference following their morning <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=meeting&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>meeting</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">议员在上午会议后与参议院代表团一同参加了大会。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The conference ended with a joint <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=meeting&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>meeting</strong></a> of the two chambers of parliament, including the senate.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">大会以议会两院联席会议结束，其中包括参议院。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a committee meeting</span> · 委员会会议</p>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-033">
<summary><h4 id="word-15-033" class="vocabulary-word-title">headquarters<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-15-033" aria-label="定位 headquarters">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="15-033" data-mark-label="headquarters" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="headquarters：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="headquarters：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=headquarters&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 headquarters" title="在有道查询 headquarters">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">15-033</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.总部，总公司；司令部</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A delegation visited police <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=headquarters&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>headquarters</strong></a> on behalf of local residents.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">代表团代表当地居民到访警察总部。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The police received the delegation at <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=headquarters&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>headquarters</strong></a> and accepted a letter on behalf of the commissioner.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">警方在总部接待代表团，并代表警察局长收下信件。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Speaking on behalf of the delegation, she thanked the police for hosting the meeting at their <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=headquarters&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>headquarters</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她代表代表团发言，感谢警方在总部主持会议。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">party headquarters</span> · 政党总部</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=representative&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">representative</a></span> · 代表</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=public%20relations&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">public relations</a></span> · 公共关系</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-034">
<summary><h4 id="word-15-034" class="vocabulary-word-title">delegation<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-15-034" aria-label="定位 delegation">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="15-034" data-mark-label="delegation" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="delegation：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="delegation：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=delegation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 delegation" title="在有道查询 delegation">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">15-034</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.代表团；委派</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=delegation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>delegation</strong></a> visited police headquarters on behalf of local residents.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">代表团代表当地居民到访警察总部。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The police received the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=delegation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>delegation</strong></a> at headquarters and accepted a letter on behalf of the commissioner.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">警方在总部接待代表团，并代表警察局长收下信件。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Speaking on behalf of the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=delegation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>delegation</strong></a>, she thanked the police for hosting the meeting at their headquarters.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她代表代表团发言，感谢警方在总部主持会议。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">an official delegation</span> · 官方代表团</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=representative&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">representative</a></span> · 代表</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=public%20relations&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">public relations</a></span> · 公共关系</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-035">
<summary><h4 id="word-15-035" class="vocabulary-word-title">behalf<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-15-035" aria-label="定位 behalf">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="15-035" data-mark-label="behalf" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="behalf：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="behalf：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=behalf&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 behalf" title="在有道查询 behalf">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">15-035</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. [on～of sb. )代表某人</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A delegation visited police headquarters on <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=behalf&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>behalf</strong></a> of local residents.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">代表团代表当地居民到访警察总部。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The police received the delegation at headquarters and accepted a letter on <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=behalf&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>behalf</strong></a> of the commissioner.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">警方在总部接待代表团，并代表警察局长收下信件。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Speaking on <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=behalf&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>behalf</strong></a> of the delegation, she thanked the police for hosting the meeting at their headquarters.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她代表代表团发言，感谢警方在总部主持会议。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">on behalf of</span> · 代表</p>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-036">
<summary><h4 id="word-15-036" class="vocabulary-word-title">police<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-15-036" aria-label="定位 police">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="15-036" data-mark-label="police" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="police：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="police：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=police&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 police" title="在有道查询 police">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">15-036</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 警察；［the～］警方</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A delegation visited <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=police&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>police</strong></a> headquarters on behalf of local residents.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">代表团代表当地居民到访警察总部。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=police&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>police</strong></a> received the delegation at headquarters and accepted a letter on behalf of the commissioner.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">警方在总部接待代表团，并代表警察局长收下信件。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Speaking on behalf of the delegation, she thanked the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=police&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>police</strong></a> for hosting the meeting at their headquarters.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她代表代表团发言，感谢警方在总部主持会议。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">the police force</span> · 警察队伍</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=representative&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">representative</a></span> · 代表</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=public%20relations&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">public relations</a></span> · 公共关系</li></ul>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-037">
<summary><h4 id="word-15-037" class="vocabulary-word-title">statesman<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-15-037" aria-label="定位 statesman">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="15-037" data-mark-label="statesman" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="statesman：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="statesman：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=statesman&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 statesman" title="在有道查询 statesman">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">15-037</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.政治家</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=statesman&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>statesman</strong></a> began his career as mayor and remained committed to public service throughout his time in office.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这位政治家以市长职位开始职业生涯，并在任职期间始终致力于公共服务。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The mayor invited a respected <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=statesman&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>statesman</strong></a> to open a new public service office.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">市长邀请一位受尊敬的政治家为新的公共服务办公室揭幕。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">After leaving office as mayor, the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=statesman&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>statesman</strong></a> continued his service through a housing charity.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">卸任市长后，这位政治家通过住房慈善机构继续服务社会。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a respected statesman</span> · 受尊敬的政治家</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=civic%20duty&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">civic duty</a></span> · 公民责任</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=leadership&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">leadership</a></span> · 领导力</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The statesman began his career as <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mayor&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mayor</strong></a> and remained committed to public service throughout his time in office.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这位政治家以市长职位开始职业生涯，并在任职期间始终致力于公共服务。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mayor&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mayor</strong></a> invited a respected statesman to open a new public service office.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">市长邀请一位受尊敬的政治家为新的公共服务办公室揭幕。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">After leaving office as <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mayor&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mayor</strong></a>, the statesman continued his service through a housing charity.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">卸任市长后，这位政治家通过住房慈善机构继续服务社会。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a city mayor</span> · 市长</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=civic%20duty&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">civic duty</a></span> · 公民责任</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=leadership&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">leadership</a></span> · 领导力</li></ul>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The statesman began his career as mayor and remained committed to public service throughout his time in <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=office&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>office</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这位政治家以市长职位开始职业生涯，并在任职期间始终致力于公共服务。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The mayor invited a respected statesman to open a new public service <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=office&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>office</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">市长邀请一位受尊敬的政治家为新的公共服务办公室揭幕。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-041">

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.局，处，科；办事处</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The administration asked each department and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bureau&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bureau</strong></a> to harness local expertise.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">行政当局要求每个部门和局利用当地专业知识。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A new <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bureau&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bureau</strong></a> will help the department harness data to improve public administration.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">新设的局将帮助该部门利用数据改善公共行政。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To harness public feedback, the administration created a joint <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bureau&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bureau</strong></a> within the planning department.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为利用公众反馈，行政当局在规划部门内设立了联合办事处。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a statistics bureau</span> · 统计局</p>

</div>
</details>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The administration asked each <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=department&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>department</strong></a> and bureau to harness local expertise.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">行政当局要求每个部门和局利用当地专业知识。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A new bureau will help the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=department&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>department</strong></a> harness data to improve public administration.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">新设的局将帮助该部门利用数据改善公共行政。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To harness public feedback, the administration created a joint bureau within the planning <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=department&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>department</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为利用公众反馈，行政当局在规划部门内设立了联合办事处。</p></li>
</ol>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The administration asked each department and bureau to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=harness&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>harness</strong></a> local expertise.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">行政当局要求每个部门和局利用当地专业知识。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A new bureau will help the department <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=harness&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>harness</strong></a> data to improve public administration.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">新设的局将帮助该部门利用数据改善公共行政。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=harness&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>harness</strong></a> public feedback, the administration created a joint bureau within the planning department.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为利用公众反馈，行政当局在规划部门内设立了联合办事处。</p></li>
</ol>
