### 词群 15.04｜权力如何作用于社会组织 {#group-15-04}

**串联方式：** 描述支配、影响与重要性，再比较组织、协会、联盟、社区及联合行动。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-045">
<summary><h4 id="word-15-045" class="vocabulary-word-title">dominate<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-15-045" aria-label="定位 dominate">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="15-045" data-mark-label="dominate" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="dominate：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="dominate：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dominate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 dominate" title="在有道查询 dominate">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">15-045</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">V.支配，控制；占优势</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Groups with economic power can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dominate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>dominate</strong></a> debate and use their influence to affect policy.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">有经济实力的团体可能主导辩论，并运用影响力影响政策。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The rules limit the power of any one donor to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dominate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>dominate</strong></a> the organisation or affect decisions through private influence.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">规则限制单一捐助者主导组织或凭私人影响力左右决定的权力。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Public oversight can affect how leaders exercise power and prevent a small circle from using its influence to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dominate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>dominate</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">公众监督能影响领导人行使权力的方式，阻止小圈子凭影响力占据主导。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">dominate a debate</span> · 主导辩论</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=oversight&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">oversight</a></span> · 监督</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=lobbying&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">lobbying</a></span> · 游说</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-046">
<summary><h4 id="word-15-046" class="vocabulary-word-title">power<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-15-046" aria-label="定位 power">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="15-046" data-mark-label="power" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="power：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="power：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=power&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 power" title="在有道查询 power">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">15-046</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.能力；权力；政权；能源</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Groups with economic <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=power&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>power</strong></a> can dominate debate and use their influence to affect policy.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">有经济实力的团体可能主导辩论，并运用影响力影响政策。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The rules limit the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=power&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>power</strong></a> of any one donor to dominate the organisation or affect decisions through private influence.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">规则限制单一捐助者主导组织或凭私人影响力左右决定的权力。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Public oversight can affect how leaders exercise <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=power&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>power</strong></a> and prevent a small circle from using its influence to dominate.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">公众监督能影响领导人行使权力的方式，阻止小圈子凭影响力占据主导。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">political power</span> · 政治权力</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=oversight&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">oversight</a></span> · 监督</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=lobbying&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">lobbying</a></span> · 游说</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-047">
<summary><h4 id="word-15-047" class="vocabulary-word-title">influence<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-15-047" aria-label="定位 influence">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="15-047" data-mark-label="influence" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="influence：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="influence：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=influence&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 influence" title="在有道查询 influence">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">15-047</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 影响，作用；v.影响</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Groups with economic power can dominate debate and use their <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=influence&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>influence</strong></a> to affect policy.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">有经济实力的团体可能主导辩论，并运用影响力影响政策。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The rules limit the power of any one donor to dominate the organisation or affect decisions through private <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=influence&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>influence</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">规则限制单一捐助者主导组织或凭私人影响力左右决定的权力。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Public oversight can affect how leaders exercise power and prevent a small circle from using its <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=influence&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>influence</strong></a> to dominate.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">公众监督能影响领导人行使权力的方式，阻止小圈子凭影响力占据主导。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">exert influence</span> · 施加影响</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=oversight&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">oversight</a></span> · 监督</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=lobbying&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">lobbying</a></span> · 游说</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-048">
<summary><h4 id="word-15-048" class="vocabulary-word-title">affect<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-15-048" aria-label="定位 affect">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="15-048" data-mark-label="affect" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="affect：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="affect：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=affect&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 affect" title="在有道查询 affect">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">15-048</span></span></summary>

<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 影响；(感情上)打动，使悲伤</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Groups with economic power can dominate debate and use their influence to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=affect&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>affect</strong></a> policy.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">有经济实力的团体可能主导辩论，并运用影响力影响政策。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The rules limit the power of any one donor to dominate the organisation or <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=affect&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>affect</strong></a> decisions through private influence.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">规则限制单一捐助者主导组织或凭私人影响力左右决定的权力。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Public oversight can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=affect&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>affect</strong></a> how leaders exercise power and prevent a small circle from using its influence to dominate.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">公众监督能影响领导人行使权力的方式，阻止小圈子凭影响力占据主导。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">affect policy</span> · 影响政策</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=oversight&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">oversight</a></span> · 监督</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=lobbying&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">lobbying</a></span> · 游说</li></ul>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-049">
<summary><h4 id="word-15-049" class="vocabulary-word-title">importance<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-15-049" aria-label="定位 importance">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="15-049" data-mark-label="importance" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="importance：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="importance：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=importance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 importance" title="在有道查询 importance">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">15-049</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.重要，重要性</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The residents’ association stressed the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=importance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>importance</strong></a> of the vote and its significance for the whole organisation.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">居民协会强调了投票的重要性及其对整个组织的意义。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The organisation recognised the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=importance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>importance</strong></a> of the association’s work and the wider significance of its campaign.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">组织认识到协会工作的重要性及其运动的更广泛意义。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The association explained the significance of the report, while the organisation highlighted its <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=importance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>importance</strong></a> for funding decisions.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">协会解释了报告的意义，组织则强调其对资金决定的重要性。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">recognise the importance</span> · 认识到重要性</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=membership&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">membership</a></span> · 会员资格</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=advocacy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">advocacy</a></span> · 倡议</li></ul>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-053">

<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.联合；联盟；工会</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=union&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>union</strong></a> hopes to unite workers and the local community around a housing campaign.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">工会希望围绕住房运动团结工人与当地社区。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A community meeting helped the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=union&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>union</strong></a> unite members behind the new proposal.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">社区会议帮助工会团结会员支持新提案。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=union&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>union</strong></a> and community leaders will unite to demand safer workplaces.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">工会与社区领袖将团结起来，要求更安全的工作场所。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a trade union</span> · 工会</p>

</details>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=community&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>community</strong></a> meeting helped the union unite members behind the new proposal.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">社区会议帮助工会团结会员支持新提案。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The union and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=community&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>community</strong></a> leaders will unite to demand safer workplaces.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">工会与社区领袖将团结起来，要求更安全的工作场所。</p></li>

<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.财团，联营企业</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A research institution joined the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=consortium&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>consortium</strong></a> with support from a league of local councils.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一家研究机构在地方议会联盟支持下加入联合体。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The league invited each institution in the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=consortium&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>consortium</strong></a> to share its findings.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">联盟邀请联合体中的每家机构分享研究发现。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Funding from the league allowed the institution to lead the new <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=consortium&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>consortium</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">联盟提供的资金让该机构得以牵头新联合体。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a research consortium</span> · 研究联合体</p>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A research <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=institution&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>institution</strong></a> joined the consortium with support from a league of local councils.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一家研究机构在地方议会联盟支持下加入联合体。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The league invited each <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=institution&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>institution</strong></a> in the consortium to share its findings.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">联盟邀请联合体中的每家机构分享研究发现。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Funding from the league allowed the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=institution&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>institution</strong></a> to lead the new consortium.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">联盟提供的资金让该机构得以牵头新联合体。</p></li>
</ol>

</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-058">
<summary><h4 id="word-15-058" class="vocabulary-word-title">unite<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-15-058" aria-label="定位 unite">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="15-058" data-mark-label="unite" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="unite：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="unite：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=unite&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 unite" title="在有道查询 unite">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">15-058</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The union hopes to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=unite&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>unite</strong></a> workers and the local community around a housing campaign.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">工会希望围绕住房运动团结工人与当地社区。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A community meeting helped the union <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=unite&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>unite</strong></a> members behind the new proposal.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">社区会议帮助工会团结会员支持新提案。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The union and community leaders will <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=unite&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>unite</strong></a> to demand safer workplaces.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">工会与社区领袖将团结起来，要求更安全的工作场所。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">unite around a goal</span> · 围绕目标团结</p>

### 词群 15.05｜国家与跨境流动 {#group-15-05}

**串联方式：** 从国家独特性、全球、联邦和海外进入公民事务，再区分移出与移入。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-059">

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj.独一无二的，唯一的；独特的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Each nation has a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=unique&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>unique</strong></a> role in global cooperation on problems that demand worldwide action.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">每个国家在处理需要全球行动的问题时，都在全球合作中发挥独特作用。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A nation can preserve its <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=unique&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>unique</strong></a> traditions while taking part in global trade and worldwide cultural exchange.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">国家可以保留独特传统，同时参与全球贸易和世界文化交流。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The nation’s <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=unique&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>unique</strong></a> approach attracted worldwide attention during the global summit.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">该国的独特做法在全球峰会期间引起世界关注。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a unique identity</span> · 独特身份</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=international&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">international</a></span> · 国际的</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sovereignty&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">sovereignty</a></span> · 主权</li></ul>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-060">

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Each <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=nation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>nation</strong></a> has a unique role in global cooperation on problems that demand worldwide action.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">每个国家在处理需要全球行动的问题时，都在全球合作中发挥独特作用。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=nation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>nation</strong></a> can preserve its unique traditions while taking part in global trade and worldwide cultural exchange.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">国家可以保留独特传统，同时参与全球贸易和世界文化交流。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=nation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>nation</strong></a>’s unique approach attracted worldwide attention during the global summit.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">该国的独特做法在全球峰会期间引起世界关注。</p></li>
</ol>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=federal&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>federal</strong></a> office helps overseas citizens understand civil rights and foreign regulations while living abroad.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">联邦办事处帮助海外公民了解在国外生活时的公民权利和外国规定。</p></li>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The federal office helps <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=overseas&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>overseas</strong></a> citizens understand civil rights and foreign regulations while living abroad.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">联邦办事处帮助海外公民了解在国外生活时的公民权利和外国规定。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Federal officials discussed civil protection for workers abroad with foreign employers and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=overseas&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>overseas</strong></a> agencies.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">联邦官员与外国雇主和海外机构讨论了对海外工作者的民事保护。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The federal guide explains civil registration for citizens abroad, including foreign documents issued <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=overseas&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>overseas</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">联邦指南解释了海外公民的民事登记，包括在海外签发的外国证件。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">overseas residents</span> · 海外居民</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=consular&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">consular</a></span> · 领事的</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=citizenship&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">citizenship</a></span> · 公民身份</li></ul>

</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-068">
<summary><h4 id="word-15-068" class="vocabulary-word-title">emigrate<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-15-068" aria-label="定位 emigrate">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="15-068" data-mark-label="emigrate" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="emigrate：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="emigrate：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=emigrate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 emigrate" title="在有道查询 emigrate">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">15-068</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.移居国外</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">They plan to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=emigrate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>emigrate</strong></a> from their home country and immigrate to Canada.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">他们计划离开祖国，移居加拿大。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">People who <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=emigrate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>emigrate</strong></a> for work must meet the rules of the country they hope to immigrate to.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为工作移居国外的人必须满足目标移入国的规定。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">She chose to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=emigrate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>emigrate</strong></a> after university and later helped her parents immigrate to the same country.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她大学毕业后选择移居国外，后来又帮助父母移入同一国家。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">emigrate from a country</span> · 移出某国</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=migration&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">migration</a></span> · 移民迁移</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=residence%20permit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">residence permit</a></span> · 居留许可</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-069">
<summary><h4 id="word-15-069" class="vocabulary-word-title">immigrate<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-15-069" aria-label="定位 immigrate">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="15-069" data-mark-label="immigrate" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="immigrate：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="immigrate：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=immigrate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 immigrate" title="在有道查询 immigrate">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">15-069</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. (从外地)移居，移入</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">They plan to emigrate from their home country and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=immigrate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>immigrate</strong></a> to Canada.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">他们计划离开祖国，移居加拿大。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">People who emigrate for work must meet the rules of the country they hope to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=immigrate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>immigrate</strong></a> to.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为工作移居国外的人必须满足目标移入国的规定。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">She chose to emigrate after university and later helped her parents <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=immigrate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>immigrate</strong></a> to the same country.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她大学毕业后选择移居国外，后来又帮助父母移入同一国家。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">immigrate to a country</span> · 移入某国</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=migration&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">migration</a></span> · 移民迁移</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=residence%20permit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">residence permit</a></span> · 居留许可</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

### 词群 15.06｜统治、殖民与独立 {#group-15-06}

**串联方式：** 由王位、王冠及统治进入殖民与独立；soviet、New Zealand、Germany 用作历史或地理词形辨认。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-070">
<summary><h4 id="word-15-070" class="vocabulary-word-title">reign<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-15-070" aria-label="定位 reign">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="15-070" data-mark-label="reign" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="reign：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="reign：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reign&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 reign" title="在有道查询 reign">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">15-070</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 君主统治时期；v. 统治</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">During his <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reign&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>reign</strong></a>, the king wore the crown and occupied the throne, but critics called him a puppet.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">国王统治期间戴着王冠、坐着王位，但批评者称他为傀儡。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The novel follows a puppet ruler whose <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reign&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>reign</strong></a> ends when he loses both the crown and the throne.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">小说讲述一位傀儡统治者的故事，他失去王冠和王位时，统治也随之结束。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A crown does not guarantee real power: the heir to the throne remained a puppet throughout his <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reign&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>reign</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">王冠不保证实权：这位王位继承人在整个统治期间仍是傀儡。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a long reign</span> · 漫长统治期</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=monarchy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">monarchy</a></span> · 君主制</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=royal%20succession&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">royal succession</a></span> · 王位继承</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-071">
<summary><h4 id="word-15-071" class="vocabulary-word-title">puppet<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-15-071" aria-label="定位 puppet">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="15-071" data-mark-label="puppet" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="puppet：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="puppet：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=puppet&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 puppet" title="在有道查询 puppet">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">15-071</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 玩偶；傀儡</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">During his reign, the king wore the crown and occupied the throne, but critics called him a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=puppet&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>puppet</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">国王统治期间戴着王冠、坐着王位，但批评者称他为傀儡。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The novel follows a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=puppet&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>puppet</strong></a> ruler whose reign ends when he loses both the crown and the throne.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">小说讲述一位傀儡统治者的故事，他失去王冠和王位时，统治也随之结束。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A crown does not guarantee real power: the heir to the throne remained a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=puppet&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>puppet</strong></a> throughout his reign.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">王冠不保证实权：这位王位继承人在整个统治期间仍是傀儡。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a puppet ruler</span> · 傀儡统治者</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=monarchy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">monarchy</a></span> · 君主制</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=royal%20succession&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">royal succession</a></span> · 王位继承</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">During his reign, the king wore the crown and occupied the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=throne&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>throne</strong></a>, but critics called him a puppet.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">国王统治期间戴着王冠、坐着王位，但批评者称他为傀儡。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The novel follows a puppet ruler whose reign ends when he loses both the crown and the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=throne&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>throne</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">小说讲述一位傀儡统治者的故事，他失去王冠和王位时，统治也随之结束。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A crown does not guarantee real power: the heir to the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=throne&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>throne</strong></a> remained a puppet throughout his reign.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">王冠不保证实权：这位王位继承人在整个统治期间仍是傀儡。</p></li>
</ol>

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.花环，花冠；(祭奠用的)花圈</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">They laid a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wreath&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>wreath</strong></a> at the Soviet war memorial.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">他们在苏联战争纪念碑前献了花圈。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A faded photograph showed a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wreath&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>wreath</strong></a> beside a Soviet soldier’s grave.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一张褪色照片展示了苏联士兵墓旁的一只花圈。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The ceremony included a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wreath&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>wreath</strong></a> for those commemorated by the Soviet-era monument.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">仪式包括为这座苏联时期纪念碑所纪念的人献花圈。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">lay a wreath</span> · 献花圈</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=commemoration&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">commemoration</a></span> · 纪念活动</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=war%20memorial&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">war memorial</a></span> · 战争纪念碑</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">They laid a wreath at the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=soviet&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Soviet</strong></a> war memorial.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">他们在苏联战争纪念碑前献了花圈。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A faded photograph showed a wreath beside a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=soviet&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Soviet</strong></a> soldier’s grave.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一张褪色照片展示了苏联士兵墓旁的一只花圈。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The ceremony included a wreath for those commemorated by the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=soviet&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Soviet</strong></a>-era monument.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">仪式包括为这座苏联时期纪念碑所纪念的人献花圈。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">Soviet archives</span> · 苏联档案</p>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-078">
<summary><h4 id="word-15-078" class="vocabulary-word-title">colony<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-15-078" aria-label="定位 colony">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="15-078" data-mark-label="colony" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="colony：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="colony：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=colony&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 colony" title="在有道查询 colony">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">15-078</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.殖民地</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=colony&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>colony</strong></a>’s campaign for liberty eventually became a movement for independence.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">殖民地争取自由的运动最终变成了独立运动。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">After independence, the former <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=colony&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>colony</strong></a> debated how to protect individual liberty.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">独立后，前殖民地讨论了如何保护个人自由。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The historian examined whether independence brought greater liberty to all groups in the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=colony&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>colony</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">历史学家考察独立是否给殖民地的所有群体带来了更多自由。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a former colony</span> · 前殖民地</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=self-government&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">self-government</a></span> · 自治</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=decolonisation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">decolonisation</a></span> · 非殖民化</li></ul>

</details>

### 词群 15.07｜人口、身份与庇护 {#group-15-07}

**串联方式：** 从贫民区、避难和庇护进入人口统计、公民居民、族群与宗族。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-081">
<summary><h4 id="word-15-081" class="vocabulary-word-title">slum<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-15-081" aria-label="定位 slum">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="15-081" data-mark-label="slum" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="slum：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="slum：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=slum&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 slum" title="在有道查询 slum">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">15-081</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.贫民窟</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Families seeking refuge from conflict lived in a crowded <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=slum&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>slum</strong></a> while applying for asylum.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">逃离冲突寻求避难的家庭，在申请庇护期间住在拥挤贫民窟。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The shelter offered refuge to asylum applicants who had been sleeping in a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=slum&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>slum</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">避难所为此前露宿贫民窟的庇护申请人提供了庇护。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The report follows an asylum seeker who found temporary refuge in a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=slum&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>slum</strong></a> near the border.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">报告追踪了一位在边境附近贫民窟找到临时庇护的庇护申请者。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a crowded slum</span> · 拥挤的贫民窟</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=displacement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">displacement</a></span> · 流离失所</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=refugee&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">refugee</a></span> · 难民</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-082">
<summary><h4 id="word-15-082" class="vocabulary-word-title">refuge<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-15-082" aria-label="定位 refuge">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="15-082" data-mark-label="refuge" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="refuge：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="refuge：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=refuge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 refuge" title="在有道查询 refuge">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">15-082</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Families seeking <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=refuge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>refuge</strong></a> from conflict lived in a crowded slum while applying for asylum.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">逃离冲突寻求避难的家庭，在申请庇护期间住在拥挤贫民窟。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The shelter offered <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=refuge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>refuge</strong></a> to asylum applicants who had been sleeping in a slum.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">避难所为此前露宿贫民窟的庇护申请人提供了庇护。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The report follows an asylum seeker who found temporary <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=refuge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>refuge</strong></a> in a slum near the border.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">报告追踪了一位在边境附近贫民窟找到临时庇护的庇护申请者。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-084">
<summary><h4 id="word-15-084" class="vocabulary-word-title">population<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-15-084" aria-label="定位 population">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="15-084" data-mark-label="population" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="population：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="population：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=population&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 population" title="在有道查询 population">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">15-084</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.人口，人口数量</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The demographic survey counts every resident in the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=population&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>population</strong></a>, whether or not that person is a citizen.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">人口调查统计人口中的每位居民，无论其是否为公民。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A resident may be included in <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=population&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>population</strong></a> statistics without being a citizen, which matters in demographic analysis.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">居民即使不是公民，也可能计入人口统计，这对人口分析很重要。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The citizen panel asked how demographic change would affect services for each resident as the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=population&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>population</strong></a> aged.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">公民小组询问，随着人口老龄化，人口变化会如何影响每位居民获得的服务。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">population growth</span> · 人口增长</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=census&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">census</a></span> · 人口普查</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ageing&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">ageing</a></span> · 老龄化</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-085">
<summary><h4 id="word-15-085" class="vocabulary-word-title">demographic<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-15-085" aria-label="定位 demographic">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="15-085" data-mark-label="demographic" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="demographic：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="demographic：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=demographic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 demographic" title="在有道查询 demographic">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">15-085</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj.人口(学)的，人口统计(学)的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=demographic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>demographic</strong></a> survey counts every resident in the population, whether or not that person is a citizen.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">人口调查统计人口中的每位居民，无论其是否为公民。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A resident may be included in population statistics without being a citizen, which matters in <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=demographic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>demographic</strong></a> analysis.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">居民即使不是公民，也可能计入人口统计，这对人口分析很重要。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The citizen panel asked how <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=demographic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>demographic</strong></a> change would affect services for each resident as the population aged.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">公民小组询问，随着人口老龄化，人口变化会如何影响每位居民获得的服务。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">demographic change</span> · 人口变化</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=census&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">census</a></span> · 人口普查</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ageing&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">ageing</a></span> · 老龄化</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-088">
<summary><h4 id="word-15-088" class="vocabulary-word-title">ethnic<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-15-088" aria-label="定位 ethnic">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="15-088" data-mark-label="ethnic" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="ethnic：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="ethnic：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ethnic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 ethnic" title="在有道查询 ethnic">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">15-088</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj.民族的，种族的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The study distinguishes clan membership from <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ethnic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>ethnic</strong></a> identity and racial classification.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">研究区分了宗族归属、族群认同和种族分类。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A clan may share an <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ethnic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>ethnic</strong></a> tradition, but that does not justify racial stereotypes.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">宗族可能共享族群传统，但这不能为种族刻板印象提供理由。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The museum explored clan history while explaining how <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ethnic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>ethnic</strong></a> labels differ from racial categories.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">博物馆探讨宗族历史，同时解释族群标签与种族类别的区别。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">an ethnic minority</span> · 少数族群</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=identity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">identity</a></span> · 认同</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=heritage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">heritage</a></span> · 传承</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-089">
<summary><h4 id="word-15-089" class="vocabulary-word-title">racial<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-15-089" aria-label="定位 racial">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="15-089" data-mark-label="racial" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="racial：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="racial：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=racial&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 racial" title="在有道查询 racial">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">15-089</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj. 种族的，人种的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The study distinguishes clan membership from ethnic identity and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=racial&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>racial</strong></a> classification.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">研究区分了宗族归属、族群认同和种族分类。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A clan may share an ethnic tradition, but that does not justify <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=racial&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>racial</strong></a> stereotypes.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">宗族可能共享族群传统，但这不能为种族刻板印象提供理由。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The museum explored clan history while explaining how ethnic labels differ from <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=racial&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>racial</strong></a> categories.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">博物馆探讨宗族历史，同时解释族群标签与种族类别的区别。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">racial discrimination</span> · 种族歧视</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=identity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">identity</a></span> · 认同</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=heritage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">heritage</a></span> · 传承</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

### 词群 15.08｜选举怎样产生继任者 {#group-15-08}

**串联方式：** 先看选举权与资格，再看偏好、投票、民调、提名、核对名单与继任。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-091">
<summary><h4 id="word-15-091" class="vocabulary-word-title">franchise<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-15-091" aria-label="定位 franchise">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="15-091" data-mark-label="franchise" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="franchise：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="franchise：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=franchise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 franchise" title="在有道查询 franchise">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">15-091</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 选举权；特许经营权（商业语境）</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Extending the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=franchise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>franchise</strong></a> would entitle more citizens to vote according to their preference and elect a representative.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">扩大选举权将赋予更多公民按偏好投票并选出代表的权利。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=franchise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>franchise</strong></a> rules entitle eligible residents to vote, express a preference and elect the council.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">选举权规则赋予符合资格的居民投票、表达偏好和选举议会的权利。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Reformers want a wider <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=franchise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>franchise</strong></a> to entitle young adults to vote and elect leaders who reflect their preference.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">改革者希望扩大选举权，让年轻成年人有权投票并选出符合其偏好的领导人。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">extend the franchise</span> · 扩大选举权</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ballot&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">ballot</a></span> · 选票</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=electorate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">electorate</a></span> · 选民群体</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-092">
<summary><h4 id="word-15-092" class="vocabulary-word-title">entitle<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-15-092" aria-label="定位 entitle">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="15-092" data-mark-label="entitle" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="entitle：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="entitle：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=entitle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 entitle" title="在有道查询 entitle">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">15-092</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 给......权利(或资格)；给……题名</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Extending the franchise would <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=entitle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>entitle</strong></a> more citizens to vote according to their preference and elect a representative.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">扩大选举权将赋予更多公民按偏好投票并选出代表的权利。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The franchise rules <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=entitle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>entitle</strong></a> eligible residents to vote, express a preference and elect the council.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">选举权规则赋予符合资格的居民投票、表达偏好和选举议会的权利。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Reformers want a wider franchise to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=entitle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>entitle</strong></a> young adults to vote and elect leaders who reflect their preference.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">改革者希望扩大选举权，让年轻成年人有权投票并选出符合其偏好的领导人。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">entitle someone to vote</span> · 赋予某人投票权</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ballot&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">ballot</a></span> · 选票</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=electorate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">electorate</a></span> · 选民群体</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-093">

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.偏爱；优先权</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Extending the franchise would entitle more citizens to vote according to their <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=preference&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>preference</strong></a> and elect a representative.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">扩大选举权将赋予更多公民按偏好投票并选出代表的权利。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The franchise rules entitle eligible residents to vote, express a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=preference&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>preference</strong></a> and elect the council.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">选举权规则赋予符合资格的居民投票、表达偏好和选举议会的权利。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Reformers want a wider franchise to entitle young adults to vote and elect leaders who reflect their <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=preference&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>preference</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">改革者希望扩大选举权，让年轻成年人有权投票并选出符合其偏好的领导人。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a political preference</span> · 政治偏好</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ballot&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">ballot</a></span> · 选票</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=electorate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">electorate</a></span> · 选民群体</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-094">

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 选举权；投票；v. 表决；投票</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Extending the franchise would entitle more citizens to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=vote&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>vote</strong></a> according to their preference and elect a representative.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">扩大选举权将赋予更多公民按偏好投票并选出代表的权利。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The franchise rules entitle eligible residents to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=vote&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>vote</strong></a>, express a preference and elect the council.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">选举权规则赋予符合资格的居民投票、表达偏好和选举议会的权利。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Reformers want a wider franchise to entitle young adults to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=vote&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>vote</strong></a> and elect leaders who reflect their preference.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">改革者希望扩大选举权，让年轻成年人有权投票并选出符合其偏好的领导人。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">cast a vote</span> · 投票</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ballot&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">ballot</a></span> · 选票</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=electorate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">electorate</a></span> · 选民群体</li></ul>
</div>
