<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-095">
<summary><h4 id="word-15-095" class="vocabulary-word-title">elect<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-15-095" aria-label="定位 elect">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="15-095" data-mark-label="elect" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="elect：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="elect：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=elect&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 elect" title="在有道查询 elect">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">15-095</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.选举；选择</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Extending the franchise would entitle more citizens to vote according to their preference and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=elect&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>elect</strong></a> a representative.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">扩大选举权将赋予更多公民按偏好投票并选出代表的权利。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The franchise rules entitle eligible residents to vote, express a preference and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=elect&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>elect</strong></a> the council.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">选举权规则赋予符合资格的居民投票、表达偏好和选举议会的权利。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Reformers want a wider franchise to entitle young adults to vote and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=elect&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>elect</strong></a> leaders who reflect their preference.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">改革者希望扩大选举权，让年轻成年人有权投票并选出符合其偏好的领导人。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">elect a representative</span> · 选代表</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ballot&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">ballot</a></span> · 选票</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=electorate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">electorate</a></span> · 选民群体</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-096">
<summary><h4 id="word-15-096" class="vocabulary-word-title">respondent<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-15-096" aria-label="定位 respondent">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="15-096" data-mark-label="respondent" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="respondent：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="respondent：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=respondent&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 respondent" title="在有道查询 respondent">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">15-096</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. (民意测验等的)调查对象</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">One <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=respondent&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>respondent</strong></a> in the poll said the party should nominate a candidate with more ambition.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一位民调受访者表示，政党应提名一位更有抱负的候选人。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The poll asked each <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=respondent&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>respondent</strong></a> whether the leader’s ambition would persuade them to nominate her again.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">民调询问每位受访者，领导人的抱负是否会让他们再次提名她。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before they nominate anyone, organisers will poll each <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=respondent&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>respondent</strong></a> about the skills and ambition a candidate needs.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">提名任何人之前，组织者将调查每位受访者对候选人所需技能和抱负的看法。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a survey respondent</span> · 调查受访者</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=candidacy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">candidacy</a></span> · 候选资格</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=public%20opinion&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">public opinion</a></span> · 公众意见</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-097">
<summary><h4 id="word-15-097" class="vocabulary-word-title">poll<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-15-097" aria-label="定位 poll">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="15-097" data-mark-label="poll" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="poll：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="poll：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=poll&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 poll" title="在有道查询 poll">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">15-097</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.民意调查；选举投票；投票数</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">One respondent in the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=poll&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>poll</strong></a> said the party should nominate a candidate with more ambition.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一位民调受访者表示，政党应提名一位更有抱负的候选人。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=poll&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>poll</strong></a> asked each respondent whether the leader’s ambition would persuade them to nominate her again.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">民调询问每位受访者，领导人的抱负是否会让他们再次提名她。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before they nominate anyone, organisers will <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=poll&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>poll</strong></a> each respondent about the skills and ambition a candidate needs.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">提名任何人之前，组织者将调查每位受访者对候选人所需技能和抱负的看法。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">an opinion poll</span> · 民意调查</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=candidacy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">candidacy</a></span> · 候选资格</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=public%20opinion&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">public opinion</a></span> · 公众意见</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-098">
<summary><h4 id="word-15-098" class="vocabulary-word-title">ambition<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-15-098" aria-label="定位 ambition">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="15-098" data-mark-label="ambition" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="ambition：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="ambition：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ambition&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 ambition" title="在有道查询 ambition">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">15-098</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.雄心，抱负</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">One respondent in the poll said the party should nominate a candidate with more <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ambition&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>ambition</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一位民调受访者表示，政党应提名一位更有抱负的候选人。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The poll asked each respondent whether the leader’s <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ambition&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>ambition</strong></a> would persuade them to nominate her again.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">民调询问每位受访者，领导人的抱负是否会让他们再次提名她。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before they nominate anyone, organisers will poll each respondent about the skills and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ambition&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>ambition</strong></a> a candidate needs.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">提名任何人之前，组织者将调查每位受访者对候选人所需技能和抱负的看法。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">political ambition</span> · 政治抱负</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=candidacy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">candidacy</a></span> · 候选资格</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=public%20opinion&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">public opinion</a></span> · 公众意见</li></ul>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-099">
<summary><h4 id="word-15-099" class="vocabulary-word-title">nominate<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-15-099" aria-label="定位 nominate">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="15-099" data-mark-label="nominate" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="nominate：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="nominate：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=nominate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 nominate" title="在有道查询 nominate">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">15-099</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.提名；任命</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">One respondent in the poll said the party should <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=nominate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>nominate</strong></a> a candidate with more ambition.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一位民调受访者表示，政党应提名一位更有抱负的候选人。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The poll asked each respondent whether the leader’s ambition would persuade them to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=nominate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>nominate</strong></a> her again.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">民调询问每位受访者，领导人的抱负是否会让他们再次提名她。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before they <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=nominate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>nominate</strong></a> anyone, organisers will poll each respondent about the skills and ambition a candidate needs.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">提名任何人之前，组织者将调查每位受访者对候选人所需技能和抱负的看法。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">nominate a candidate</span> · 提名候选人</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=candidacy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">candidacy</a></span> · 候选资格</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=public%20opinion&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">public opinion</a></span> · 公众意见</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-100">
<summary><h4 id="word-15-100" class="vocabulary-word-title">checklist<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-15-100" aria-label="定位 checklist">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="15-100" data-mark-label="checklist" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="checklist：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="checklist：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=checklist&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 checklist" title="在有道查询 checklist">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">15-100</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=checklist&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>checklist</strong></a> covers every stage of the succession plan.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">清单涵盖接班计划的每个阶段。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A clear succession procedure and a practical <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=checklist&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>checklist</strong></a> helped the organisation choose a new leader.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">清晰的继任程序和实用清单帮助组织选出新领导人。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The board reviewed the succession <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=checklist&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>checklist</strong></a> before the chair retired.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">主席退休前，董事会审阅了接班清单。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a planning checklist</span> · 计划清单</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=transition&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">transition</a></span> · 交接</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=successor&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">successor</a></span> · 继任者</li></ul>

### 词群 15.09｜公共治理希望实现什么 {#group-15-09}

**串联方式：** 用安全、福利、福祉、和谐、稳定和繁荣描述目标，再表达前景与不同视角。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-102">
<summary><h4 id="word-15-102" class="vocabulary-word-title">safety<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-15-102" aria-label="定位 safety">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="15-102" data-mark-label="safety" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="safety：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="safety：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=safety&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 safety" title="在有道查询 safety">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">15-102</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.安全；安全场所</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Public <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=safety&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>safety</strong></a> and social welfare can support well-being and harmony in a community.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">公共安全和社会福利能促进社区福祉与和谐。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The policy aims to improve <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=safety&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>safety</strong></a>, protect welfare and strengthen well-being without disrupting community harmony.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">政策旨在改善安全、保障福利和增进福祉，同时避免破坏社区和谐。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Residents linked their well-being to reliable welfare services, personal <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=safety&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>safety</strong></a> and harmony among neighbours.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">居民把福祉与可靠福利服务、人身安全及邻里和谐联系起来。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">public safety</span> · 公共安全</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=quality%20of%20life&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">quality of life</a></span> · 生活质量</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=social%20cohesion&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">social cohesion</a></span> · 社会凝聚力</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-103">
<summary><h4 id="word-15-103" class="vocabulary-word-title">welfare<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-15-103" aria-label="定位 welfare">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="15-103" data-mark-label="welfare" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="welfare：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="welfare：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=welfare&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 welfare" title="在有道查询 welfare">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">15-103</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.健康，幸福</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Public safety and social <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=welfare&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>welfare</strong></a> can support well-being and harmony in a community.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">公共安全和社会福利能促进社区福祉与和谐。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The policy aims to improve safety, protect <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=welfare&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>welfare</strong></a> and strengthen well-being without disrupting community harmony.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">政策旨在改善安全、保障福利和增进福祉，同时避免破坏社区和谐。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Residents linked their well-being to reliable <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=welfare&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>welfare</strong></a> services, personal safety and harmony among neighbours.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">居民把福祉与可靠福利服务、人身安全及邻里和谐联系起来。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">social welfare</span> · 社会福利</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=quality%20of%20life&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">quality of life</a></span> · 生活质量</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=social%20cohesion&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">social cohesion</a></span> · 社会凝聚力</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-104">
<summary><h4 id="word-15-104" class="vocabulary-word-title">well-being<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-15-104" aria-label="定位 well-being">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="15-104" data-mark-label="well-being" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="well-being：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="well-being：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=well-being&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 well-being" title="在有道查询 well-being">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">15-104</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.康乐，安宁</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Public safety and social welfare can support <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=well-being&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>well-being</strong></a> and harmony in a community.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">公共安全和社会福利能促进社区福祉与和谐。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The policy aims to improve safety, protect welfare and strengthen <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=well-being&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>well-being</strong></a> without disrupting community harmony.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">政策旨在改善安全、保障福利和增进福祉，同时避免破坏社区和谐。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Residents linked their <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=well-being&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>well-being</strong></a> to reliable welfare services, personal safety and harmony among neighbours.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">居民把福祉与可靠福利服务、人身安全及邻里和谐联系起来。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">mental well-being</span> · 心理健康</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=quality%20of%20life&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">quality of life</a></span> · 生活质量</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=social%20cohesion&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">social cohesion</a></span> · 社会凝聚力</li></ul>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-105">
<summary><h4 id="word-15-105" class="vocabulary-word-title">harmony<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-15-105" aria-label="定位 harmony">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="15-105" data-mark-label="harmony" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="harmony：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="harmony：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=harmony&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 harmony" title="在有道查询 harmony">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">15-105</span></span></summary>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Public safety and social welfare can support well-being and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=harmony&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>harmony</strong></a> in a community.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">公共安全和社会福利能促进社区福祉与和谐。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The policy aims to improve safety, protect welfare and strengthen well-being without disrupting community <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=harmony&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>harmony</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">政策旨在改善安全、保障福利和增进福祉，同时避免破坏社区和谐。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Residents linked their well-being to reliable welfare services, personal safety and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=harmony&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>harmony</strong></a> among neighbours.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">居民把福祉与可靠福利服务、人身安全及邻里和谐联系起来。</p></li>
</ol>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-106">
<summary><h4 id="word-15-106" class="vocabulary-word-title">steady<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-15-106" aria-label="定位 steady">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="15-106" data-mark-label="steady" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="steady：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="steady：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=steady&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 steady" title="在有道查询 steady">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">15-106</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj.不摇晃的，平稳的；稳步的；稳定的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=steady&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Steady</strong></a> investment gives local firms a better prospect of being able to flourish and succeed.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">稳定投资让当地企业更有希望繁荣并取得成功。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The prospect of <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=steady&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>steady</strong></a> demand encouraged businesses to invest, flourish and succeed.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">对稳定需求的预期鼓励企业投资、兴旺并取得成功。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">For the scheme to succeed and the town to flourish, planners need <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=steady&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>steady</strong></a> funding and a realistic prospect of jobs.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">要让方案成功、小镇兴旺，规划者需要稳定资金，以及现实的就业前景。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">steady growth</span> · 稳定增长</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=prosperity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">prosperity</a></span> · 繁荣</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=opportunity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">opportunity</a></span> · 机会</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-107">

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 繁荣，兴旺；茁壮成长</p>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Steady investment gives local firms a better prospect of being able to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=flourish&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>flourish</strong></a> and succeed.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">稳定投资让当地企业更有希望繁荣并取得成功。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The prospect of steady demand encouraged businesses to invest, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=flourish&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>flourish</strong></a> and succeed.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">对稳定需求的预期鼓励企业投资、兴旺并取得成功。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">For the scheme to succeed and the town to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=flourish&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>flourish</strong></a>, planners need steady funding and a realistic prospect of jobs.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">要让方案成功、小镇兴旺，规划者需要稳定资金，以及现实的就业前景。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">businesses flourish</span> · 企业繁荣</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=prosperity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">prosperity</a></span> · 繁荣</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=opportunity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">opportunity</a></span> · 机会</li></ul>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Steady investment gives local firms a better prospect of being able to flourish and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=succeed&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>succeed</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">稳定投资让当地企业更有希望繁荣并取得成功。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The prospect of steady demand encouraged businesses to invest, flourish and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=succeed&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>succeed</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">对稳定需求的预期鼓励企业投资、兴旺并取得成功。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">For the scheme to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=succeed&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>succeed</strong></a> and the town to flourish, planners need steady funding and a realistic prospect of jobs.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">要让方案成功、小镇兴旺，规划者需要稳定资金，以及现实的就业前景。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">succeed in a task</span> · 成功完成任务</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=prosperity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">prosperity</a></span> · 繁荣</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=opportunity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">opportunity</a></span> · 机会</li></ul>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-109">
<summary><h4 id="word-15-109" class="vocabulary-word-title">prospect<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-15-109" aria-label="定位 prospect">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="15-109" data-mark-label="prospect" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="prospect：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="prospect：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=prospect&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 prospect" title="在有道查询 prospect">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">15-109</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.希望，可能性；风景</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Steady investment gives local firms a better <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=prospect&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>prospect</strong></a> of being able to flourish and succeed.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">稳定投资让当地企业更有希望繁荣并取得成功。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=prospect&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>prospect</strong></a> of steady demand encouraged businesses to invest, flourish and succeed.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">对稳定需求的预期鼓励企业投资、兴旺并取得成功。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">For the scheme to succeed and the town to flourish, planners need steady funding and a realistic <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=prospect&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>prospect</strong></a> of jobs.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">要让方案成功、小镇兴旺，规划者需要稳定资金，以及现实的就业前景。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a realistic prospect</span> · 现实的希望</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=prosperity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">prosperity</a></span> · 繁荣</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=opportunity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">opportunity</a></span> · 机会</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-110">
<summary><h4 id="word-15-110" class="vocabulary-word-title">perspective<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-15-110" aria-label="定位 perspective">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="15-110" data-mark-label="perspective" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="perspective：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="perspective：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=perspective&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 perspective" title="在有道查询 perspective">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">15-110</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.景点；观点</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">From a legal standpoint, her viewpoint was reasonable, but a wider <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=perspective&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>perspective</strong></a> changed her optimistic outlook.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">从法律立场看，她的观点合理，但更广阔视角改变了她原本乐观的看法。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The report presents the resident’s viewpoint, the planner’s <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=perspective&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>perspective</strong></a> and the council’s standpoint before offering a cautious outlook.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">报告先呈现居民观点、规划者视角和议会立场，再提出谨慎展望。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A social <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=perspective&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>perspective</strong></a> and an economic standpoint may produce a different viewpoint on the same regional outlook.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">社会视角与经济立场可能对同一区域前景产生不同观点。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a long-term perspective</span> · 长远视角</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=assessment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">assessment</a></span> · 评估</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=interpretation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">interpretation</a></span> · 解读</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-111">
<summary><h4 id="word-15-111" class="vocabulary-word-title">viewpoint<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-15-111" aria-label="定位 viewpoint">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="15-111" data-mark-label="viewpoint" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="viewpoint：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="viewpoint：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=viewpoint&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 viewpoint" title="在有道查询 viewpoint">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">15-111</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.观点，看法；视角</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">From a legal standpoint, her <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=viewpoint&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>viewpoint</strong></a> was reasonable, but a wider perspective changed her optimistic outlook.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">从法律立场看，她的观点合理，但更广阔视角改变了她原本乐观的看法。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The report presents the resident’s <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=viewpoint&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>viewpoint</strong></a>, the planner’s perspective and the council’s standpoint before offering a cautious outlook.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">报告先呈现居民观点、规划者视角和议会立场，再提出谨慎展望。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A social perspective and an economic standpoint may produce a different <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=viewpoint&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>viewpoint</strong></a> on the same regional outlook.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">社会视角与经济立场可能对同一区域前景产生不同观点。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a different viewpoint</span> · 不同观点</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=assessment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">assessment</a></span> · 评估</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=interpretation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">interpretation</a></span> · 解读</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-112">
<summary><h4 id="word-15-112" class="vocabulary-word-title">standpoint<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-15-112" aria-label="定位 standpoint">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="15-112" data-mark-label="standpoint" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="standpoint：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="standpoint：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=standpoint&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 standpoint" title="在有道查询 standpoint">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">15-112</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.立场，立足点</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">From a legal <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=standpoint&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>standpoint</strong></a>, her viewpoint was reasonable, but a wider perspective changed her optimistic outlook.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">从法律立场看，她的观点合理，但更广阔视角改变了她原本乐观的看法。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The report presents the resident’s viewpoint, the planner’s perspective and the council’s <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=standpoint&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>standpoint</strong></a> before offering a cautious outlook.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">报告先呈现居民观点、规划者视角和议会立场，再提出谨慎展望。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A social perspective and an economic <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=standpoint&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>standpoint</strong></a> may produce a different viewpoint on the same regional outlook.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">社会视角与经济立场可能对同一区域前景产生不同观点。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">from a legal standpoint</span> · 从法律立场看</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=assessment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">assessment</a></span> · 评估</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=interpretation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">interpretation</a></span> · 解读</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-113">
<summary><h4 id="word-15-113" class="vocabulary-word-title">outlook<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-15-113" aria-label="定位 outlook">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="15-113" data-mark-label="outlook" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="outlook：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="outlook：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=outlook&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 outlook" title="在有道查询 outlook">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">15-113</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. (对生活的)观点；展望，前景</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">From a legal standpoint, her viewpoint was reasonable, but a wider perspective changed her optimistic <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=outlook&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>outlook</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">从法律立场看，她的观点合理，但更广阔视角改变了她原本乐观的看法。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The report presents the resident’s viewpoint, the planner’s perspective and the council’s standpoint before offering a cautious <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=outlook&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>outlook</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">报告先呈现居民观点、规划者视角和议会立场，再提出谨慎展望。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A social perspective and an economic standpoint may produce a different viewpoint on the same regional <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=outlook&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>outlook</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">社会视角与经济立场可能对同一区域前景产生不同观点。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">an optimistic outlook</span> · 乐观的看法</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=assessment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">assessment</a></span> · 评估</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=interpretation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">interpretation</a></span> · 解读</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

### 词群 15.10｜政策如何提出和公开 {#group-15-10}

**串联方式：** 从指引、伦理和建议进入提案，再用宣布、声称、声明和澄清表达立场。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-114">
<summary><h4 id="word-15-114" class="vocabulary-word-title">guideline<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-15-114" aria-label="定位 guideline">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="15-114" data-mark-label="guideline" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="guideline：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="guideline：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=guideline&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 guideline" title="在有道查询 guideline">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">15-114</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.指导方针</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=guideline&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>guideline</strong></a> asks advisers to suggest improvements to each proposal and advise officials according to a public-service ethic.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">指导原则要求顾问根据公共服务伦理，对每份提案建议改进并为官员提供意见。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A strong public-service ethic should guide those who suggest a proposal or advise on a new <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=guideline&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>guideline</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">强烈的公共服务伦理应指导提出提案或对新指导原则提供意见的人。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Experts advise the board to revise the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=guideline&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>guideline</strong></a> and suggest that every proposal should reflect an ethic of fairness.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">专家建议董事会修改指导原则，并提出每份提案都应体现公平伦理。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a policy guideline</span> · 政策指导原则</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=recommendation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">recommendation</a></span> · 建议</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=integrity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">integrity</a></span> · 廉正</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-115">

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The guideline asks advisers to suggest improvements to each proposal and advise officials according to a public-service <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ethic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>ethic</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">指导原则要求顾问根据公共服务伦理，对每份提案建议改进并为官员提供意见。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A strong public-service <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ethic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>ethic</strong></a> should guide those who suggest a proposal or advise on a new guideline.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">强烈的公共服务伦理应指导提出提案或对新指导原则提供意见的人。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Experts advise the board to revise the guideline and suggest that every proposal should reflect an <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ethic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>ethic</strong></a> of fairness.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">专家建议董事会修改指导原则，并提出每份提案都应体现公平伦理。</p></li>
</ol>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A strong public-service ethic should guide those who <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=suggest&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>suggest</strong></a> a proposal or advise on a new guideline.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">强烈的公共服务伦理应指导提出提案或对新指导原则提供意见的人。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Experts advise the board to revise the guideline and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=suggest&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>suggest</strong></a> that every proposal should reflect an ethic of fairness.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">专家建议董事会修改指导原则，并提出每份提案都应体现公平伦理。</p></li>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The guideline asks advisers to suggest improvements to each proposal and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=advise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>advise</strong></a> officials according to a public-service ethic.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">指导原则要求顾问根据公共服务伦理，对每份提案建议改进并为官员提供意见。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A strong public-service ethic should guide those who suggest a proposal or <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=advise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>advise</strong></a> on a new guideline.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">强烈的公共服务伦理应指导提出提案或对新指导原则提供意见的人。</p></li>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The guideline asks advisers to suggest improvements to each <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=proposal&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>proposal</strong></a> and advise officials according to a public-service ethic.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">指导原则要求顾问根据公共服务伦理，对每份提案建议改进并为官员提供意见。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A strong public-service ethic should guide those who suggest a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=proposal&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>proposal</strong></a> or advise on a new guideline.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">强烈的公共服务伦理应指导提出提案或对新指导原则提供意见的人。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Experts advise the board to revise the guideline and suggest that every <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=proposal&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>proposal</strong></a> should reflect an ethic of fairness.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">专家建议董事会修改指导原则，并提出每份提案都应体现公平伦理。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-119">
<summary><h4 id="word-15-119" class="vocabulary-word-title">hint<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-15-119" aria-label="定位 hint">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="15-119" data-mark-label="hint" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="hint：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="hint：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hint&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 hint" title="在有道查询 hint">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">15-119</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v./n.暗示，提示</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hint&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hint</strong></a> of agreement was not enough: leaders had to declare their support, affirm their commitment and explain their claim.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一点同意暗示并不够：领导人必须宣布支持、重申承诺并解释其主张。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The statement gave a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hint&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hint</strong></a> that officials might declare victory, affirm the results and claim a mandate.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">声明暗示官员可能宣布胜利、确认结果并声称获得授权。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before you declare the project a success or claim credit, affirm the evidence rather than relying on a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hint&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hint</strong></a> of progress.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">宣布项目成功或自称有功前，要确认证据，不要只依赖一点进展迹象。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a subtle hint</span> · 微妙暗示</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=statement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">statement</a></span> · 声明</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=evidence&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">evidence</a></span> · 证据</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-120">
<summary><h4 id="word-15-120" class="vocabulary-word-title">declare<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-15-120" aria-label="定位 declare">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="15-120" data-mark-label="declare" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="declare：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="declare：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=declare&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 declare" title="在有道查询 declare">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">15-120</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 宣布，宣告；宣称，声称</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A hint of agreement was not enough: leaders had to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=declare&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>declare</strong></a> their support, affirm their commitment and explain their claim.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一点同意暗示并不够：领导人必须宣布支持、重申承诺并解释其主张。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The statement gave a hint that officials might <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=declare&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>declare</strong></a> victory, affirm the results and claim a mandate.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">声明暗示官员可能宣布胜利、确认结果并声称获得授权。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before you <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=declare&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>declare</strong></a> the project a success or claim credit, affirm the evidence rather than relying on a hint of progress.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">宣布项目成功或自称有功前，要确认证据，不要只依赖一点进展迹象。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">declare an intention</span> · 宣布意图</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=statement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">statement</a></span> · 声明</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=evidence&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">evidence</a></span> · 证据</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-121">

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The statement gave a hint that officials might declare victory, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=affirm&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>affirm</strong></a> the results and claim a mandate.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">声明暗示官员可能宣布胜利、确认结果并声称获得授权。</p></li>

</ol>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A hint of agreement was not enough: leaders had to declare their support, affirm their commitment and explain their <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=claim&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>claim</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一点同意暗示并不够：领导人必须宣布支持、重申承诺并解释其主张。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The statement gave a hint that officials might declare victory, affirm the results and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=claim&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>claim</strong></a> a mandate.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">声明暗示官员可能宣布胜利、确认结果并声称获得授权。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-123">
<summary><h4 id="word-15-123" class="vocabulary-word-title">proclaim<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-15-123" aria-label="定位 proclaim">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="15-123" data-mark-label="proclaim" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="proclaim：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="proclaim：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=proclaim&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 proclaim" title="在有道查询 proclaim">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">15-123</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.声明；清楚地表明</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The council will announce the decision, state its reasons, clarify the rules and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=proclaim&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>proclaim</strong></a> its commitment to openness.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">议会将宣布决定、说明理由、澄清规则，并公开表明开放承诺。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before leaders <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=proclaim&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>proclaim</strong></a> success, they should state the results, announce any limitations and clarify what remains unfinished.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">领导人宣布成功前，应陈述结果、公布局限，并说明尚未完成之处。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The spokesperson will clarify the agreement, state the timetable and announce when the assembly intends to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=proclaim&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>proclaim</strong></a> the new charter.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">发言人将澄清协议、说明时间表，并宣布大会何时拟颁布新宪章。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">proclaim independence</span> · 宣布独立</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=public%20announcement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">public announcement</a></span> · 公告</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=transparency&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">transparency</a></span> · 透明度</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-124">
<summary><h4 id="word-15-124" class="vocabulary-word-title">state<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-15-124" aria-label="定位 state">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="15-124" data-mark-label="state" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="state：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="state：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=state&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 state" title="在有道查询 state">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">15-124</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.陈述，说明；n.国家；州；状况</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The council will announce the decision, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=state&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>state</strong></a> its reasons, clarify the rules and proclaim its commitment to openness.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">议会将宣布决定、说明理由、澄清规则，并公开表明开放承诺。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before leaders proclaim success, they should <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=state&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>state</strong></a> the results, announce any limitations and clarify what remains unfinished.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">领导人宣布成功前，应陈述结果、公布局限，并说明尚未完成之处。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The spokesperson will clarify the agreement, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=state&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>state</strong></a> the timetable and announce when the assembly intends to proclaim the new charter.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">发言人将澄清协议、说明时间表，并宣布大会何时拟颁布新宪章。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">state a position</span> · 陈述立场</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=public%20announcement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">public announcement</a></span> · 公告</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=transparency&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">transparency</a></span> · 透明度</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-125">
<summary><h4 id="word-15-125" class="vocabulary-word-title">announce<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-15-125" aria-label="定位 announce">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="15-125" data-mark-label="announce" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="announce：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="announce：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=announce&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 announce" title="在有道查询 announce">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">15-125</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The council will <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=announce&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>announce</strong></a> the decision, state its reasons, clarify the rules and proclaim its commitment to openness.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">议会将宣布决定、说明理由、澄清规则，并公开表明开放承诺。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before leaders proclaim success, they should state the results, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=announce&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>announce</strong></a> any limitations and clarify what remains unfinished.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">领导人宣布成功前，应陈述结果、公布局限，并说明尚未完成之处。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The spokesperson will clarify the agreement, state the timetable and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=announce&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>announce</strong></a> when the assembly intends to proclaim the new charter.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">发言人将澄清协议、说明时间表，并宣布大会何时拟颁布新宪章。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">announce a decision</span> · 宣布决定</p>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-126">
<summary><h4 id="word-15-126" class="vocabulary-word-title">clarify<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-15-126" aria-label="定位 clarify">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="15-126" data-mark-label="clarify" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="clarify：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="clarify：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=clarify&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 clarify" title="在有道查询 clarify">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">15-126</span></span></summary>

### 词群 15.11｜政策如何落地 {#group-15-11}

**串联方式：** 援助和鼓励之后，实施并监督；admit 的承认、准许进入等义项依语境区分。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-127">
<summary><h4 id="word-15-127" class="vocabulary-word-title">assist<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-15-127" aria-label="定位 assist">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="15-127" data-mark-label="assist" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="assist：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="assist：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=assist&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 assist" title="在有道查询 assist">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">15-127</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 帮助，协助</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Volunteers <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=assist&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>assist</strong></a> residents, distribute aid and encourage families to use local services.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">志愿者帮助居民、发放救援物资，并鼓励家庭使用本地服务。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The aid programme will <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=assist&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>assist</strong></a> small charities and encourage cooperation between them.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">援助项目将帮助小型慈善机构，并鼓励它们合作。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Officials encourage trained volunteers to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=assist&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>assist</strong></a> with aid distribution.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">官员鼓励受训志愿者协助发放救援物资。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">assist residents</span> · 帮助居民</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=support&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">support</a></span> · 支持</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=relief&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">relief</a></span> · 救济</li></ul>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The aid programme will assist small charities and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=encourage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>encourage</strong></a> cooperation between them.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">援助项目将帮助小型慈善机构，并鼓励它们合作。</p></li>
