<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-130">
<summary><h4 id="word-15-130" class="vocabulary-word-title">implement<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-15-130" aria-label="定位 implement">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="15-130" data-mark-label="implement" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="implement：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="implement：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=implement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 implement" title="在有道查询 implement">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">15-130</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.实施，执行；n.工具，器具</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Officials must <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=implement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>implement</strong></a> the policy, monitor its effects and admit mistakes when they occur.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">官员必须执行政策、监测效果，并在出错时承认错误。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The agency agreed to monitor results and admit problems as it began to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=implement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>implement</strong></a> the scheme.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">机构开始执行方案时，同意监测结果并承认问题。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">If we <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=implement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>implement</strong></a> changes without resources to monitor them, we should admit that evaluation will be limited.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">如果实施改变却没有监测资源，就应承认评估会受到限制。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">implement a policy</span> · 执行政策</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=evaluation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">evaluation</a></span> · 评估</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=accountability&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">accountability</a></span> · 问责</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-131">
<summary><h4 id="word-15-131" class="vocabulary-word-title">monitor<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-15-131" aria-label="定位 monitor">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="15-131" data-mark-label="monitor" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="monitor：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="monitor：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=monitor&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 monitor" title="在有道查询 monitor">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">15-131</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 监视；监听；n. 监控器；监视器；班长</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Officials must implement the policy, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=monitor&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>monitor</strong></a> its effects and admit mistakes when they occur.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">官员必须执行政策、监测效果，并在出错时承认错误。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The agency agreed to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=monitor&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>monitor</strong></a> results and admit problems as it began to implement the scheme.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">机构开始执行方案时，同意监测结果并承认问题。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">If we implement changes without resources to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=monitor&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>monitor</strong></a> them, we should admit that evaluation will be limited.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">如果实施改变却没有监测资源，就应承认评估会受到限制。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">monitor progress</span> · 监测进展</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=evaluation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">evaluation</a></span> · 评估</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=accountability&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">accountability</a></span> · 问责</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-132">
<summary><h4 id="word-15-132" class="vocabulary-word-title">admit<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-15-132" aria-label="定位 admit">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="15-132" data-mark-label="admit" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="admit：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="admit：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=admit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 admit" title="在有道查询 admit">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">15-132</span></span></summary>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Officials must implement the policy, monitor its effects and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=admit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>admit</strong></a> mistakes when they occur.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">官员必须执行政策、监测效果，并在出错时承认错误。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The agency agreed to monitor results and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=admit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>admit</strong></a> problems as it began to implement the scheme.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">机构开始执行方案时，同意监测结果并承认问题。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">If we implement changes without resources to monitor them, we should <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=admit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>admit</strong></a> that evaluation will be limited.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">如果实施改变却没有监测资源，就应承认评估会受到限制。</p></li>

### 词群 15.12｜辨认地区、国家与相关形容词 {#group-15-12}

**串联方式：** 把地区和国家名称与相关形容词分开；本组混有人群、语言与文化称呼，不一律译成国籍。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="15-133">
<summary><h4 id="word-15-133" class="vocabulary-word-title">African<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-15-133" aria-label="定位 African">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="15-133" data-mark-label="African" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="African：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="African：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=African&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 African" title="在有道查询 African">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">15-133</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj.非洲的；n.非洲人</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The library holds <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=African&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>African</strong></a> literature, European history, Latin poetry, Jewish memoirs and studies of the Arabian Peninsula.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">图书馆藏有非洲文学、欧洲历史、拉丁诗歌、犹太回忆录及阿拉伯半岛研究资料。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The cultural fair presented <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=African&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>African</strong></a> and European music, Latin readings, Jewish storytelling and a map of Arabian trade routes.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">文化节展示了非洲与欧洲音乐、拉丁文朗读、犹太故事和阿拉伯贸易路线图。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Scholars compared <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=African&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>African</strong></a> and European sources with Latin texts, Jewish archives and travel records from the Arabian Peninsula.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">学者把非洲和欧洲资料与拉丁文文本、犹太档案及阿拉伯半岛旅行记录作比较。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">African literature</span> · 非洲文学</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cultural%20history&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">cultural history</a></span> · 文化史</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=regional%20studies&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">regional studies</a></span> · 区域研究</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj.欧洲的；n.欧洲人</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The museum catalogue describes a Roman coin, a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=Portuguese&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Portuguese</strong></a> chart, a Russian icon, a Spanish painting and a Swiss clock.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">博物馆图录介绍了罗马硬币、葡萄牙海图、俄罗斯圣像、西班牙绘画和瑞士钟表。</p></li>

### 本章 Language Takeaway

优先尝试主动说出的三个表达：

- **implement a policy**：实施政策；policy 为补充词。
- **promote public welfare**：促进公共福利；promote 来自 promotion 的词族。
- **monitor the results**：监督结果。

### 串学示范｜自拟短文

A government makes decisions that affect residents and communities. Officials may propose a reform, clarify its purpose and implement new guidelines. They can then monitor the results and respond to public concerns. Discussions about safety, welfare and equality often involve different viewpoints and priorities.

**中文理解：** 政府的决定会影响居民和社区。官员可以提出改革、澄清目的并实施新指引，随后监督结果并回应公众关切。关于安全、福利和平等的讨论，经常涉及不同观点与优先事项。

### 闭卷回忆｜先尝试，再逐级提示

**任务：** 选择一项公共议题：哪些机构参与，谁受影响，怎样提议、实施与监督？

1. 关掉本章正文，在空白页列出你记得的词群；每群先写 3—5 个英文词或搭配。
2. 从中选 3 个词群，连成 3—5 句英文；用明确关系连接，专业分类和事实不确定时先核对。
3. 卡住时，先只看“本章主线”；还想不起，再看词群标题和中文简释，最后才看英文。
4. 用核对编号找出遗漏词；把“见到认得”与“想表达时能说出”分开记录。
5. 合上提示重新说一遍；下次换一个对象、地点或例子，检查能否迁移。
